FROM (
    SELECT TOP(@p) [o].[Menu_ItemId] AS [MenuItemId], COALESCE(SUM([o].[Quantity]), 0) AS [UnitsSold]

) AS [o0]

SELECT [m].[MenuItemId], [m].[Available], [m].[CategoryId], [m].[CreatedAt], [m].[CreatedBy], [m].[Description], [m].[ImagePath], [m].[IsActive], [m].[Name], [m].[Price], [m].[UpdatedAt], [m].[UpdatedBy]
FROM (
    SELECT TOP(@p) [o].[Menu_ItemId] AS [MenuItemId], COALESCE(SUM([o].[Quantity]), 0) AS [UnitsSold]
    FROM [OrderDetails] AS [o]
    GROUP BY [o].[Menu_ItemId]
    ORDER BY COALESCE(SUM([o].[Quantity]), 0) DESC
) AS [o0]
INNER JOIN [MenuItems] AS [m] ON [o0].[MenuItemId] = [m].[MenuItemId]

ORDER BY [o0].[UnitsSold] DESC
